#457 · id 360 INTERNSHIP Urgent 29-07-2026 12:02
CDC Internship process 2026-27
Kind attention: CDC Internship registered students: a) The internship interview process will be conducted at Nalanda only. b) Keep your pen, notepad, copy of your CV (at least 6/7 copies), all electronic gadgets handy. CDC Office will not entertain any requests from the candidates to print out their CVs during the process. c) Keep one Govt ID card, Institute ID card handy. d) Dress code is formal. e) As the process is being conducted online / offline / hybrid mode , your process may be preponed or postponed. Hence be available in mobile and check CDC notice board. CDC office/ Placecom member may call you at any time. Be vigilant. f) You are advised to behave properly with courtesy with the company officials, placecom and CDC office. g) Student should refrain themselves to indulge in any malpractices which may tarnish the image of the Institute. If CDC receives any complaint from the company suitable disciplinary action will be taken as per Institute/ CDC policy. h) CDC office will publish the shortlist in advance in CDC Notice board (ERP). The students are advised to keep checking CDC Notice Board (in ERP) at regular intervals. i) Students having multiple shortlists will be accommodated in a particular way, so that they can attend the same. The same will be communicated in advance. Be vigilant and attend the phone calls, whatsapp messages from Placecom/ CDC office. j) No student is allowed to disclose any information pertaining to the date of interview, the time of interview, the roles offered, the stipend offered, and the eligibility criteria of any company while interviewing for, or interacting with any other company. In the event of such a happening, CDC will consider it as violation and appropriate action will be taken. k) CDC office will publish the internship process result in CDC notice board. Single offer will be treated as DEEMED ACCEPTED. Offer acceptance will only be taken from those who has multiple offers. l) All the students will have to accept the offer in ERP (be it single offer or multiple offer). It is MANDATORY. Please note that once you get a CDC internship offer, you will not be allowed to sit in any further CDC internship process. m) Any kind of discrepancies in the name/roll number or issues in the declared list may be brought to the notice of CDC on the same day. CDC helpline number -03222-281870 (only available on the days of interview, during office hours). CDC, IIT Kharagpur

Notice regarding malpractice during Internship process 2026-2027
All students are hereby informed that the upcoming internship process (2026-2027) shall be conducted with the highest standards of honesty, integrity, and professionalism. Any form of malpractice, including but not limited to providing false information, impersonation, unfair means, misrepresentation, bypassing test guidelines or any unethical conduct during the internship process will evoke serious consequences. Students found to have engaged in any malpractice at any stage & from any source during the internship process will be debarred from participating in Day 1, Day 2 and Day 3 of the internship process. In addition, disciplinary action will be initiated against the concerned student(s) as deemed appropriate by the institute authorities, in accordance with the applicable rules and regulations.
